- **c64**: Commodore 64 demoscene optimizer that plans and reviews 6502/6510 code on any toolchain, from hand-written assembly to cc65, llvm-mos and zig-mos. Use it before writing a hot routine, a raster or interrupt scheme, a memory map or an effect, to choose the approach within a cycle and byte budget; and after changes to assembly, hot loops, raster or interrupt timing, VIC-II/CIA/SID register use or the memory map, to review them. - **ca-backlog-auditor**: Audits development backlogs at element level (each work item) and collection level (all items together). Evaluates completeness, clarity, assumptions, ordering, and gaps. - **ca-brainstormer**: Internal agent used by arc-investigate. Generates 5-6 evidence-based theories about problem causes after conducting investigative research. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-brainstormer.md) · ★1)

- **ca-data-critic**: ライブモデレーター + データ検証ゲートキーパーの専門家。 Phase 1/2 のディスカッションをモデレートし（矛盾検出・議論促進・収束判定）、 Gate 1/2 で全員参加の合意形成をファシリテートする。 Gate 2 では Devil's Advocate ラウンドと Independent Validation を運営。 確証バイアスを排除するため「望ましい結論」を知らない状態で検証する。 competitive-analysis チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/competitive-analysis/agents/ca-data-critic.md) · ★38)
- **ca-debugger**: Runs a scientific-method debugging session, maintaining .code_assist/.debug/DEBUG.md across the investigation. ([nik190799/cadence](https://github.com/nik190799/cadence/blob/HEAD/plugins/cadence/agents/cadence-feature-engineer.md))

For {{greenhouse_role}}, searches LinkedIn for candidates matching {{sourcing_criteria}}, checks the role's Greenhouse pipeline so no one already applied, contacted, or passed on gets re-surfaced, and drafts a personalized outreach email per new match to {{recruiter_review_inbox}} — capped at {{daily_cap}} candidates per run. Never sends outreach and never writes to Greenhouse; the recruiter reviews, sends, and owns the ATS. ([Karthik17-dev/Lead-Generation](https://github.com/Karthik17-dev/Lead-Generation/blob/HEAD/packages/starter/templates/marketplace/runtime/agents/candidate-sourcing.md))

- **candidate_verifier**: Independently verifies the implemented candidate against the approved test baseline. Use for TDD step 5, always in a fresh context, with artifacts only (never the implementer's report). Read-only. Can run in parallel with security_reviewer. - **candor-architect**: The Architecture Advisor (INTJ-A / Magician). - **candor-brainstorm**: The Horizon Keeper (ENTP / Explorer). - **candor-curator**: The Knowledge Archivist (ISTJ-A / Controller). - **candor-data**: The Skeptical Analyst (ISTJ-A / Analyzer). - **candor-decide**: The Executive (ESTJ-A / Captain). ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/blob/HEAD/plugins/candor/agents/candor-decide.md) · ★6)
- **candor-logic**: The Analytical Reasoner (INTP / Sage). ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/blob/HEAD/plugins/candor/agents/candor-logic.md) · ★6)

([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/blob/HEAD/plugins/candor/agents/candor-security.md) · ★6)
